主席報告
CHAIRMAN’
        S STATEMENT

本人欣然向各位股東提呈本集團二零二五年六月          I am pleased to present to Shareholders the business
三十日止之半年度業績回顧與展望。               review and outlook of the Group for the six months ended
                               30 June 2025.

二零二五年上半年，中國經濟穩中有進，經濟轉          In the first half of 2025, China’s economy remained resilient
型與高質量發展穩步推進。擴內需、促消費政策          and stable, with steady progress made in economic
帶動消費市場增速回升，消費支出成為經濟增長          transformation and high-quality development. Policies
核心動力。需求結構深度分化，高端消費階段性          to expand domestic demand and promote consumption
承壓，服務消費佔比提升，科技催生的新消費模          drove a rebound in the growth of the consumer market,
式加速滲透，折射出消費者對價值與體驗的雙重          with consumption expenditure becoming the core driver
追求日益深化。同期，購物中心競爭加劇，頭部          of economic growth. The structure of demand showed
運營商創新求變，新消費形態亮點紛呈。物管行          significant differentiation, with high-end consumption
業增速換擋但空間廣闊，存量競爭下「好服務」          facing temporary pressure, the service consumption
重要性凸顯。頭部物企以服務固根基、以精益提          increasing in proportion, and technology-driven new
效率、以能力拓邊界，通過內生重塑存量優勢、          consumption models accelerating their penetration. This
外延深挖增量價值，加速轉型並增強發展韌性。          reflected consumers’ growing pursuit of both value and
                               experience. Meanwhile, as competition among shopping
                               malls intensified, leading operators are innovating and
                               adapting, and new consumption models are emerging
                               with notable highlights. The property management
                               industry has shifted in growth rate, but it still possesses
                               vast development potential. In the intense competition of
                               existing projects, the importance of “high-quality service”
                               has been highlighted. Leading property management
                               companies were firmly committed to strengthening their
                               foundations through service, improving efficiency through
                               lean management, and expanding their boundaries through
                               capability enhancement. They accelerated transformation
                               and enhanced development resilience by internally
                               restructuring their advantages in competition on existing
                               projects and externally exploring the diverse value of new
                               growth opportunities.

上半年，本集團堅持「改革促發展」，以「穩增          In the first half of the year, the Group adhered to
長」為整體經營基調，秉持「內涵式增長+外延式         “reform-driven development,” adopted “stable growth” as
增長」的核心經營策略，以高質量發展的確定性          the overall operating orientation, upheld the core operating
應對內外部不確定性，業績表現領跑行業。期           strategy of “organic growth + extensional growth,” and
內，本集團實現綜合收入人民幣85.24 億元，同       responded to internal and external uncertainties with
比增長 6.5%，核心淨利潤人民幣 20.11 億元，    the certainty of high-quality development, achieving
同比增長15.0%，各項核心業績指標取得穩健增        industry-leading performance. During the Period, the
長。董事會決議宣派中期股息每股人民幣0.529        Group achieved consolidated revenue of RMB8.524 billion,
元，同比增長89.6%，中期派息率提升24個百分       representing a YoY increase of 6.5%, and core net profit of
點至60%，同步宣派特別股息每股人民幣0.352       RMB2.011 billion, representing a YoY increase of 15.0%,
元，大幅提升股東回報。                    with all core performance indicators recording steady
                               growth. The Board resolved to declare an interim dividend
                               of RMB0.529 per Share, representing a YoY increase
                               of 89.6% and an increase of 24 percentage points of
                               an interim dividend payout ratio to 60%, together with a
                               special dividend of RMB0.352 per Share, significantly
                               boosting Shareholders’ returns.

     遂寧萬象匯引領區域商業升級，鄭州鄭東萬象城          airport explored new commercial models, Shunde MIXC
     開業熱度創城市新高；新簽約商業輕資產外拓項          ONE achieved a milestone with 100% occupancy rate
     目6 個，其中4 個為30 個重點城市運輸導向型發      and 100% opening rate upon its launch, Suining MIXC
     展(TOD)項目，2 個為存量在營項目，整體質素       ONE led regional commercial upgrades, and the opening
     優良。截至二零二五年六月三十日，本集團旗下          of Zhengzhou Zhengdong MIXC hit a new high in the
     在營購物中心增至125座。                  city. There were six newly signed commercial light-asset
                                    expansion projects with excellent overall quality, of which
                                    four were urban transit-oriented development (TOD)

                                    shopping malls operated by the Group increased to 125.

     同比提升0.4 個百分點至68.2%。寫字樓業務完      控) was implemented in the shopping mall segment, with

                                    profit margin from property owners increased by 0.4

     管理層分析與討論
     MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS


     未來展望                           OUTLOOK
     堅持高質量的規模發展，鞏固公司的領              Adhering to High-Quality Scale Development
     先市場地位                          and Consolidating the Company ’s Leading
                                    Market Position
     本集團堅持以戰略引領規模拓展，實現商業及物          The Group will implement the strategy to lead scale
     業兩大主航道的高質量規模發展。一是堅持區域          expansion, and achieve high-quality and large scale
     深耕戰略，加大核心城市市場佔有率。緊抓現代          development of the two main segments, namely
     化城市群和都市圈加速形成組團式、網絡化發展          commercial management business and property
     格局契機，注重運用已進入城市所積累的品牌優          management business. First, the Group will adhere to its
     勢，進一步提升區域深耕密度。二是堅持賽道專          strategy to deepen engagement in regional markets and
     業化戰略，鍛造差異化競爭優勢。我們將着力服          intensify market penetration in core cities. The Group
     務城市內更多類型的空間，通過不斷完善產業生          will seize the opportunity of modern urban clusters and
     態佈局，積累面向不同空間的更加專業、綜合服          metropolitan areas to accelerate the formation of a

     核心淨利潤（非香港財務報告會計準               CORE NET PROFIT (NON-HKFRS
     則計量）                           ACCOUNTING STANDARDS MEASURE)
     為補充按照香港財務報告會計準則編製的綜合財          To supplement the consolidated financial statements,
     務報表，我們亦採用了非香港財務報告會計準           which are presented in accordance with HKFRS
     則規定的或按其呈列的核心淨利潤（非香港財務          Accounting Standards, we also use core net profit
     報告會計準則計量）作為附加財務計量。我們認          (non-HKFRS Accounting Standards measure) as an
     為，通過撇除與公司日常業務運營及管理無關的          additional financial measure, which is not required by,
     若干項目的潛在影響，該非香港財務報告會計準          or presented in accordance with HKFRS Accounting
     則的呈現可為投資者及管理層提供了解集團各期          Standards. We believe that the presentation of such
     核心業務綜合業績的有用資料。核心淨利潤（非          non-HKFRS Accounting Standards measure provides
     香港財務報告會計準則計量）界定為扣除投資物          useful information to investors and the management
     業的重估收益 ╱ 虧損、無形資產 —— 客戶關係       in understanding the consolidated results of the
     攤銷及處置、或然代價的公允價值變動收益 ╱          core operation of the Group from period to period by
     虧損及相關遞延稅項影響而調整後的本集團股東          excluding the potential impact of certain items that are
     應佔淨利潤。                         unrelated to the Company’s daily business operations
                                    and management. The core net profit (non-HKFRS
                                    Accounting Standards measure) is defined as the net

                                    properties, amortisation and disposal of intangible assets

                                    tax impact.

外匯風險                             FOREIGN CURRENCY RISK
由於本集團業務主要於中國進行，本集團主要採            As the Group’s business is mainly conducted in the PRC,
用人民幣作為結算貨幣。截至二零二五年六月             we mainly adopt RMB as the settlement currency. As of 30
三十日止，非人民幣資產及負債主要為現金港幣            June 2025, non-RMB assets and liabilities mainly included
448.7 百萬元、112,829.5 美元。其中，港幣現    cash of HKD448.7 million and US$112,829.5. Among them,
金餘額主要為已宣告但尚未派發的二零二四年末            cash balance in Hong Kong dollars primarily consisted
期股息及特別股息的儲備資金，並已於二零二五            of reserve funds set aside for the 2024 final dividend and
年七月二十三日完成派付。鑒於該等港幣現金的            special dividend, which had been declared but not yet
持有目的明確且存續期短，其面臨的外匯匯率波            distributed, and was fully paid out on 23 July 2025. Given
動風險較低且已實質消除。管理層認為，除上述            the clear purpose and short-term nature of holding such
已結算的股息派付事項外，本集團在經營方面並            cash in Hong Kong dollars, the foreign exchange rate
無重大外匯風險，人民幣匯率波動不會對本集團            fluctuation risk associated with it was relatively low and
財務狀況帶來顯著影響。本集團目前並無外幣風            has been effectively eliminated. The management believes
險對沖政策，然而，管理層將對外匯風險敞口實            that, except for the above-mentioned dividend payments
施動態監控並將根據市場環境的變化進行必要調            that have been settled, the operation of the Group was not
整。                               exposed to material foreign currency risk. No significant
                                 impact was caused by the fluctuation of RMB exchange
                                 rate on the Group’s financial position. Currently, the Group

                                 exchange risk, but the management will actively monitor

3   涉及受共同控制實體的業務合           3     MERGER ACCOUNTING FOR
    併之合併會計法                       BUSINESS COMBINATION
                                  INVOLVING ENTITIES UNDER
                                  COMMON CONTROL
    於二零二五年六月，本集團通過與華潤網            In June 2025, the Group acquired China Resources
    絡控股（深圳）有限公司訂立的股權轉讓            Networks (Shenzhen) Co., Ltd. and China Net Data
    協議收購華潤網絡（深圳）有限公司及華            Technology (Guangzhou) Co., Ltd. (“the Entities”)
    網數據科技（廣州）有限公司（「實體」），          through the equity transfer agreements with China
    總代價為人民幣121,038,000元。          Resources Networks Holdings (Shenzhen) Co., Ltd.
                                  at a total consideration of RMB121,038,000.


    由於交易所涉所有該等實體在收購前後均            As all these entities involved in the transaction are
    受中國華潤共同控制，交易視為受共同控            under common control of CRC before and after
    制下的業務合併。因此，對受共同控制業            the acquisition, the transaction is considered as
    務的業務合併應用合併會計原則。因此，            a business combination under common control.
    已編製本集團簡明綜合財務報表，猶如實            The principle of merger accounting for business
    體自受中國華潤共同控制以來為本公司的            combination involving business under common
    附屬公司。                         control has therefore been applied. As a result, the
                                  condensed consolidated financial statements of the
                                  Group have been prepared as if the Entities were
                                  the subsidiaries of the Company ever since they
                                  became under common control of CRC.


    因此，實體的業績、資產及負債需以原賬            Accordingly, the results, assets and liabilities of the
    面值計入本公司綜合財務報表內，猶如實            Entities should have been accounted for at historical
    體一直為本集團的一部份。因此，截至二            amounts in the consolidated financial statements of
    零二四年六月三十日止六個月的財務數據            the Company as if the Entities had always been part
    已重列。                          of the Group. Hence, financial figures for the six
                                  months ended 30 June 2024 have been restated.

            (a)    按公允價值計量的金融資產                                      (a)   Financial assets and liabilities
                   及負債（續）                                                  measured at fair value (continued)
                   (i)   公允價值層級（續）                                         (i)   Fair value hierarchy (continued)

                         本集團由財務部對金融工                                             The Group’s finance department is
                         具進行估值。於各報告日                                             performing valuations for the financial
                         期，財務經理分析金融工                                             instruments. At each reporting date,
                         具價值變動，並釐定估值                                             the finance manager analyses the
                         使用的主要輸入數據。估                                             movements in the values of financial
                         值由本集團審閱及批准。                                             instruments and determines the major
                                                                                 inputs applied in the valuation. The
